Get time\n    time_var = ds.variables['time']\n    # Convert time to datetime (GLM uses days since 1900-01-01 typically)\n    time_units = time_var.units\n    time_vals = time_var[:]\n\n    # Create datetime index\n    if 'days since' in time_units:\n        base_date = datetime.strptime(time_units.split('since')[1].strip(), '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')\n        time_index = [base_date + timedelta(days=float(t)) for t in time_vals]\n    else:\n        time_index = time_vals\n\n    # Get depth levels\n    z = ds.variables['z'][:]\n\n    # Get temperature\n    temp = ds.variables['temp'][:]\n\n    ds.close()\n\n    return time_index, z, temp\n\ndef read_observations(csv_file):\n    \"\"\"Read field observations.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"Reading observations from {csv_file}...\")\n    df = pd.read_csv(csv_file)\n\n    # Parse datetime\n    df['datetime'] = pd.to_datetime(df['datetime'])\n\n    return df\n\ndef interpolate_simulation_to_observations(sim_time, sim_z, sim_temp, obs_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Interpolate simulation data to match observation times and depths.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"Interpolating simulation to observation points...\")\n\n    obs_times = obs_df['datetime'].values\n    obs_depths = obs_df['depth'].values\n\n    # Create interpolated temperature array\n    sim_temp_interp = np.full(len(obs_df), np.nan)\n\n    # For each observation point, find the closest simulation time and interpolate depth\n    for i, (obs_time, obs_depth) in enumerate(zip(obs_times, obs_depths)):\n        # Find closest time index\n        time_diffs = np.abs([abs((t - obs_time).total_seconds()) for t in sim_time])\n        time_idx = np.argmin(time_diffs)\n\n        # Check if time difference is less than 6 hours (to avoid interpolation over large time gaps)\n        if time_diffs[time_idx] <= 6 * 3600:\n            # Interpolate depth\n            if obs_depth >= np.min(sim_z[time_idx, :]) and obs_depth <= np.max(sim_z[time_idx, :]):\n                # Get the temperature profile at this time\n                temp_profile = sim_temp[time_idx, :]\n\n                # Remove NaN values\n                valid_mask = ~np.isnan(temp_profile)\n                if np.sum(valid_mask) > 1:\n                    z_valid = sim_z[time_idx, valid_mask]\n                    temp_valid = temp_profile[valid_mask]\n\n                    # Interpolate\n                    if obs_depth >= np.min(z_valid) and obs_depth <= np.max(z_valid):\n                        temp_interp = np.interp(obs_depth, z_valid, temp_valid)\n                        sim_temp_interp[i] = temp_interp\n\n    return sim_temp_interp\n\ndef calculate_rmse(observed, simulated):\n    \"\"\"Calculate RMSE between observed and simulated values.\"\"\"\n    # Remove NaN pairs\n    mask = ~(np.isnan(observed) | np.isnan(simulated))\n    if np.sum(mask) == 0:\n        return np.nan\n\n    obs_clean = observed[mask]\n    sim_clean = simulated[mask]\n\n    rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((obs_clean - sim_clean) ** 2))\n    return rmse\n\ndef main():\n    # File paths\n    nc_file = '/root/output/output.nc'\n    obs_file = '/root/field_temp_oxy.csv'\n\n    # Read simulation output\n    sim_time, sim_z, sim_temp = read_glm_output(nc_file)\n\n    # Read observations\n    obs_df = read_observations(obs_file)\n\n    # Filter observations to simulation period\n    sim_start = sim_time[0]\n    sim_end = sim_time[-1]\n    obs_df = obs_df[(obs_df['datetime'] >= sim_start) & (obs_df['datetime'] <= sim_end)]\n\n    print(f\"\\nSimulation period: {sim_start} to {sim_end}\")\n    print(f\"Number of observations: {len(obs_df)}\")\n\n    # Interpolate simulation to observation points\n    sim_temp_interp = interpolate_simulation_to_observations(sim_time, sim_z, sim_temp, obs_df)\n\n    # Get observed temperatures\n    obs_temp = obs_df['temp'].values\n\n    # Calculate RMSE\n    rmse = calculate_rmse(obs_temp, sim_temp_interp)\n\n    # Calculate additional statistics\n    mask = ~(np.isnan(obs_temp) | np.isnan(sim_temp_interp))\n    if np.sum(mask) > 0:\n        obs_clean = obs_temp[mask]\n        sim_clean = sim_temp_interp[mask]\n        mae = np.mean(np.abs(obs_clean - sim_clean))\n        bias = np.mean(sim_clean - obs_clean)\n        correlation = np.corrcoef(obs_clean, sim_clean)[0, 1]\n\n        print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n        print(f\"STATISTICS SUMMARY\")\n        print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n        print(f\"RMSE: {rmse:.2f} °C\")\n        print(f\"MAE:  {mae:.2f} °C\")\n        print(f\"Bias: {bias:.2f} °C\")\n        print(f\"Correlation: {correlation:.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Number of matched points: {np.sum(mask)} out of {len(obs_temp)} observations\")\n        print(f\"{'='*60}\\n\")\n\n        # Check if RMSE is less than 2°C\n        if rmse < 2.0:\n            print(f\"✓ SUCCESS: RMSE ({rmse:.2f} °C) is less than 2.0 °C\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"✗ RMSE ({rmse:.2f} °C) exceeds 2.0 °C threshold\")\n            print(f\"  Additional adjustment of GLM parameters may be needed.\")\n\n        return rmse\n    else:\n        print(\"\\nNo valid observation points found in the simulation output.\")\n        return np.nan\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"}}],"model":"MiniMax-M2","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":144,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":721,"cache_read_input_tokens":25505},"service_tier":"standard","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"af065014-fa79-49a3-8a1f-200254ae5419","uuid":"2b8b376f-c30b-4957-b3bc-35d2e7931b89"}

Remove NaN values\n                valid_mask = ~np.isnan(temp_profile)\n                if np.sum(valid_mask) > 1:\n                    z_valid = sim_z[time_idx, valid_mask]\n                    temp_valid = temp_profile[valid_mask]\n\n                    # Interpolate\n                    if obs_depth >= np.min(z_valid) and obs_depth <= np.max(z_valid):\n                        temp_interp = np.interp(obs_depth, z_valid, temp_valid)\n                        sim_temp_interp[i] = temp_interp\n\n    return sim_temp_interp\n\ndef calculate_rmse(observed, simulated):\n    \"\"\"Calculate RMSE between observed and simulated values.\"\"\"\n    # Remove NaN pairs\n    mask = ~(np.isnan(observed) | np.isnan(simulated))\n    if np.sum(mask) == 0:\n        return np.nan\n\n    obs_clean = observed[mask]\n    sim_clean = simulated[mask]\n\n    rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((obs_clean - sim_clean) ** 2))\n    return rmse\n\ndef main():\n    # Get time\n    time_var = ds.variables['time']\n    # Convert time to datetime (GLM uses days since 1900-01-01 typically)\n    time_units = time_var.units\n    time_vals = time_var[:]\n\n    # Create datetime index\n    if 'days since' in time_units:\n        base_date = datetime.strptime(time_units.split('since')[1].strip(), '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')\n        time_index = [base_date + timedelta(days=float(t)) for t in time_vals]\n    elif 'hours since' in time_units:\n        base_date = datetime.strptime(time_units.split('since')[1].strip(), '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')\n        time_index = [base_date + timedelta(hours=float(t)) for t in time_vals]\n    else:\n        time_index = time_vals\n\n    # Create interpolated temperature array\n    sim_temp_interp = np.full(len(obs_df), np.nan)\n\n    # For each observation point, find the closest simulation time and interpolate depth\n    for i, (obs_time, obs_depth) in enumerate(zip(obs_times, obs_depths)):\n        # Find closest time index\n        time_diffs = np.abs([abs((t - obs_time).total_seconds()) for t in sim_time])\n        time_idx = np.argmin(time_diffs)\n\n        # Check if time difference is less than 6 hours (to avoid interpolation over large time gaps)\n        if time_diffs[time_idx] <= 6 * 3600:\n            # Interpolate\n                    if obs_depth >= np.min(z_valid) and obs_depth <= np.max(z_valid):\n                        temp_interp = np.interp(obs_depth, z_valid, temp_valid)\n                        sim_temp_interp[i] = temp_interp\n\n    return sim_temp_interp\n\ndef calculate_rmse(observed, simulated):\n    \"\"\"Calculate RMSE between observed and simulated values.\"\"\"\n    # Remove NaN pairs\n    mask = ~(np.isnan(observed) | np.isnan(simulated))\n    if np.sum(mask) == 0:\n        return np.nan\n\n    obs_clean = observed[mask]\n    sim_clean = simulated[mask]\n\n    rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((obs_clean - sim_clean) ** 2))\n    return rmse\n\ndef main():\n    # File paths\n    nc_file = '/root/output/output.nc'\n    obs_file = '/root/field_temp_oxy.csv'\n\n    # Read simulation output\n    sim_time, sim_z, sim_temp = read_glm_output(nc_file)\n\n    # Read observations\n    obs_df = read_observations(obs_file)\n\n    # Interpolate simulation to observation points\n    sim_temp_interp = interpolate_simulation_to_observations(sim_time, sim_z, sim_temp, obs_df)\n\n    # Get observed temperatures\n    obs_temp = obs_df['temp'].values\n\n    # Calculate RMSE\n    rmse = calculate_rmse(obs_temp, sim_temp_interp)\n\n    # Calculate additional statistics\n    mask = ~(np.isnan(obs_temp) | np.isnan(sim_temp_interp))\n    if np.sum(mask) > 0:\n        obs_clean = obs_temp[mask]\n        sim_clean = sim_temp_interp[mask]\n        mae = np.mean(np.abs(obs_clean - sim_clean))\n        bias = np.mean(sim_clean - obs_clean)\n        correlation = np.corrcoef(obs_clean, sim_clean)[0, 1]\n\n        print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n        print(f\"STATISTICS SUMMARY\")\n        print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n        print(f\"RMSE: {rmse:.2f} °C\")\n        print(f\"MAE:  {mae:.2f} °C\")\n        print(f\"Bias: {bias:.2f} °C\")\n        print(f\"Correlation: {correlation:.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Number of matched points: {np.sum(mask)} out of {len(obs_temp)} observations\")\n        print(f\"{'='*60}\\n\")\n\n        # For each observation point, find the closest simulation time and interpolate depth\n    for i, (obs_time, obs_depth) in enumerate(zip(obs_time_np, obs_depths)):\n        # Find closest time index\n        time_diffs = np.abs(sim_time_np.astype('datetime64[s]') - obs_time.astype('datetime64[s]'))\n        time_diffs = time_diffs.astype(float)  # Convert to seconds\n        time_idx = np.argmin(time_diffs)\n\n        # Check if time difference is less than 6 hours (to avoid interpolation over large time gaps)\n        if time_diffs[time_idx] <= 6 * 3600:\n            # Interpolate depth\n            if obs_depth >= np.min(sim_z[time_idx, :]) and obs_depth <= np.max(sim_z[time_idx, :]):\n                # Get the temperature profile at this time\n                temp_profile = sim_temp[time_idx, :]\n\n                # Remove NaN values\n                valid_mask = ~np.isnan(temp_profile)\n                if np.sum(valid_mask) > 1:\n                    z_valid = sim_z[time_idx, valid_mask]\n                    temp_valid = temp_profile[valid_mask]\n\n                    # Interpolate\n                    if obs_depth >= np.min(z_valid) and obs_depth <= np.max(z_valid):\n                        temp_interp = np.interp(obs_depth, z_valid, temp_valid)\n                        sim_temp_interp[i] = temp_interp\n\n    return sim_temp_interp\n\ndef calculate_rmse(observed, simulated):\n    \"\"\"Calculate RMSE between observed and simulated values.\"\"\"\n    # Get time\n    time_var = ds.variables['time']\n    time_units = time_var.units\n    time_vals = time_var[:]\n\n    # Create datetime index\n    if 'days since' in time_units:\n        base_date = datetime.strptime(time_units.split('since')[1].strip(), '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')\n        time_index = [base_date + timedelta(days=float(t)) for t in time_vals]\n    elif 'hours since' in time_units:\n        base_date = datetime.strptime(time_units.split('since')[1].strip(), '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')\n        time_index = [base_date + timedelta(hours=float(t)) for t in time_vals]\n    else:\n        time_index = time_vals\n\n    # Convert sim_time to numpy datetime64 for easier comparison\n    sim_time_np = np.array(sim_time, dtype='datetime64[s]')\n    obs_time_np = obs_times.astype('datetime64[s]')\n\n    # Create interpolated temperature array\n    sim_temp_interp = np.full(len(obs_df), np.nan)\n\n    # For each observation point, find the closest simulation time and interpolate depth\n    for i, (obs_time, obs_depth) in enumerate(zip(obs_time_np, obs_depths)):\n        # Find closest time index\n        time_diffs = np.abs(sim_time_np.astype('datetime64[s]') - obs_time.astype('datetime64[s]'))\n        time_diffs = time_diffs.astype(float)  # Convert to seconds\n        time_idx = np.argmin(time_diffs)\n\n        # Check if time difference is less than 6 hours (to avoid interpolation over large time gaps)\n        if time_diffs[time_idx] <= 6 * 3600:\n            # Interpolate depth\n            if obs_depth >= np.min(sim_z[time_idx, :]) and obs_depth <= np.max(sim_z[time_idx, :]):\n                # Get the temperature profile at this time\n                temp_profile = sim_temp[time_idx, :]\n\n                # Remove NaN values\n                valid_mask = ~np.isnan(temp_profile)\n                if np.sum(valid_mask) > 1:\n                    z_valid = sim_z[time_idx, valid_mask]\n                    temp_valid = temp_profile[valid_mask]\n\n                    # Interpolate\n                    if obs_depth >= np.min(z_valid) and obs_depth <= np.max(z_valid):\n                        temp_interp = np.interp(obs_depth, z_valid, temp_valid)\n                        sim_temp_interp[i] = temp_interp\n\n    return sim_temp_interp\n\ndef calculate_rmse(observed, simulated):\n    \"\"\"Calculate RMSE between observed and simulated values.\"\"\"\n    # Calculate RMSE\n    rmse = calculate_rmse(obs_temp, sim_temp_interp)\n\n    return rmse\n\ndef get_obs_profile(date_str, obs_file='/root/field_temp_oxy.csv'):\n    \"\"\"Get observed temperature profile for a specific date.\"\"\"\n    df = pd.read_csv(obs_file)\n    df['datetime'] = pd.to_datetime(df['datetime'])\n\n    # Convert sim_time to numpy datetime64 for easier comparison\n    sim_time_np = np.array(sim_time, dtype='datetime64[s]')\n    obs_time_np = obs_times.astype('datetime64[s]')\n\n    # Create interpolated temperature array\n    sim_temp_interp = np.full(len(obs_df), np.nan)\n\n    # For each observation point, find the closest simulation time and interpolate depth\n    for i, (obs_time, obs_depth) in enumerate(zip(obs_time_np, obs_depths)):\n        # Find closest time index\n        time_diffs = np.abs(sim_time_np.astype('datetime64[s]') - obs_time.astype('datetime64[s]'))\n        time_diffs = time_diffs.astype(float)  # Interpolate depth\n            if obs_depth >= np.min(sim_z[time_idx, :]) and obs_depth <= np.max(sim_z[time_idx, :]):\n                # Get the temperature profile at this time\n                temp_profile = sim_temp[time_idx, :]\n\n                # Remove NaN values\n                valid_mask = ~np.isnan(temp_profile)\n                if np.sum(valid_mask) > 1:\n                    z_valid = sim_z[time_idx, valid_mask]\n                    temp_valid = temp_profile[valid_mask]\n\n                    # Remove NaN pairs\n    mask = ~(np.isnan(observed) | np.isnan(simulated))\n    if np.sum(mask) == 0:\n        return np.nan\n\n    obs_clean = observed[mask]\n    sim_clean = simulated[mask]\n\n    rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((obs_clean - sim_clean) ** 2))\n    return rmse\n\ndef calculate_rmse_with_observations(nc_file, obs_file):\n    \"\"\"Calculate RMSE between simulation and observations.\"\"\"\n    #
